Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the statistical_mechanics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 543. The moduli space has dimension at most 430 with 5 connected components.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^430(S, F) is trivial. QED.
